/* Inclusão do codigo do pessoal de Risco: classificação de BHV (Cartões) para fins de IFRS9 */

PROC SQL;
CONNECT TO SYBASEIQ (&conex_syb_neon.);
	CREATE TABLE Fatobehavior AS SELECT * FROM CONNECTION TO SYBASEIQ
     (SELECT d.NuCPFCNPJ,
			 a.CdDimContaCartao,
             a.VrLimiteCreditoContaCrteM,
             a.VrSaldoFaturaM,
             a.DtCorteFaturaM,
			 a.CdEstadoContaCartaoM,
             c.nmestadocontacartao,
			 a.DtEnquadramentoCreditoLqdco
         FROM DWBI_DMOC.TbFatoApuracaoBehaviour as a
		 left join DWBI_DTWH.TbEstadoContaCartao c
           on a.CdEstadoContaCartaoM=c.cdestadocontacartao
	  	 left JOIN DWBI_DIME.TbDimPessoa AS d
		   on a.cdpessoatitularconta=d.cdpessoa
		 		 where (DtCorteFaturaM<=%str(%'&DataBase.%') and
                        DtCorteFaturaM>=%str(%'&DtBaseM6.%') or
                a.DtEnquadramentoCreditoLqdco  <> '99991231'));
QUIT;
RUN;

data Fatobehavior;
set Fatobehavior;
if substr(put(CdDimContaCartao,$10.),2,1) = '0' then CdProduto = substr(put(CdDimContaCartao,$10.),1,1)*1;
else CdProduto = substr(put(CdDimContaCartao,$11.),1,2)*1;
NuConta = CdDimContaCartao - CdProduto * 1000000000;
run;

%macro cartao_ifrs();
proc sort data=Fatobehavior;
by CdDimContaCartao CdProduto descending DtCorteFaturaM;
run;

proc sort data=Fatobehavior  out=Fatobehavior1 nodupkey;
by CdDimContaCartao CdProduto;
run;

data Fatobehavior1;
set Fatobehavior1;
contanova1= compress(NuConta || put(CdProduto,z2.))*1;
if DtEnquadramentoCreditoLqdco = '31Dec9999'd then fl_cl=0; else  fl_cl=1;

run;
%mend;
%cartao_ifrs ();

DATA BHS_CARTAO_BS2016;
	SET extrac.BEHAVIOR_CARTAO;
	WHERE DTPROCESSBS >="&m6aa."d and DTPROCESSBS<="&DtBasep."d;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=BHS_CARTAO_BS2016 OUT=BHS_CARTAO_BS2016_3C  NODUPKEY;
  BY NUCPFCNPJ CDPRODUTO CONTA  DESCENDING base;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=BHS_CARTAO_BS2016_3C  OUT=BHS_CARTAO_BS2016_3D  NODUPKEY;
  BY NUCPFCNPJ CONTA CDPRODUTO;
RUN;

/*proc sql;*/
/*create table BHS_CARTAO_bs1 as (*/
/*select a.*, */
/*case when b.GH > 0 then b.GH */
/*     else c.GH      end as GH*/
/*from BHS_CARTAO_BS2016_3D as  a*/
/*left join extrac.BHS_CARTAO_REVOLVER as b*/
/*on a.Conta=b.nuconta and a.cdproduto=b.cdproduto  and a.base=b.base*/
/*left join extrac.BHS_CARTAO_TRANSACTOR as c*/
/*on a.Conta=c.nuconta and a.cdproduto=c.cdproduto  and a.base=c.base);*/
/* As bases BHS_CARTAO_REVOLVER e BHS_CARTAO_TRANSACTOR foram criadas pontualmente pelo Wag ano passado no diretorio
'/riscosbvf/modelos/parametros', diretorio de origem do libname 'extrac' e portanto não vai refletir pontuações mais
recentes do Behavior */

proc sql;
create table BHS_CARTAO_bs1 as (
select a.*,
case
when (a.cpto = "REVOLVER") then b1.GH
when (a.cpto = "TRANSACTOR") then b2.GH
end as GH

from BHS_CARTAO_BS2016_3D as  a
left join extrac.PARAMETROS_PD_BHS_CARTOES as b1 on b1.Modelo = "Revolver" and a.PD_BS = b1.PD12
left join extrac.PARAMETROS_PD_BHS_CARTOES as b2 on b2.Modelo = "Transactor" and a.PD_BS = b2.PD12
);quit;

proc sql;
create table BHS_CARTAO_bs1 as (
select a.*,
case
when (a.cpto = "REVOLVER") then b1.EAD
when (a.cpto = "TRANSACTOR") then b2.EAD
when (a.cpto = "NAOSCORADO") then b3.EAD
when (a.cpto = "INDETERM") then b4.EAD
end as EAD_aptos,
case
when (a.cpto = "REVOLVER") then b1.LGD
when (a.cpto = "TRANSACTOR") then b2.LGD
when (a.cpto = "NAOSCORADO") then b3.LGD
when (a.cpto = "INDETERM") then b4.LGD
end as LGD_aptos

from BHS_CARTAO_bs1 as  a
left join extrac.PARAMETROS_PD_BHS_CARTOES as b1 on b1.Modelo = "Revolver" and a.PD_BS = b1.PD12
left join extrac.PARAMETROS_PD_BHS_CARTOES as b2 on b2.Modelo = "Transactor" and a.PD_BS = b2.PD12
left join extrac.PARAMETROS_PD_BHS_CARTOES as b3 on b3.Modelo = "NAOSCORADO" and a.PD_BS = b3.PD12
left join extrac.PARAMETROS_PD_BHS_CARTOES as b4 on b4.Modelo = "INDETERM" and a.PD_BS = b4.PD12
);quit;

data BHS_CARTAO_bs1(drop = EAD_aptos LGD_aptos);
set BHS_CARTAO_bs1;
if EAD = . then EAD = EAD_aptos;
if LGD = . then LGD = LGD_aptos;
run;

/* Contingencia caso os parametros ainda vem como missing */
data BHS_CARTAO_bs1;
set BHS_CARTAO_bs1;
if EAD = . then EAD = 1.34;
if LGD = . then LGD = 0.71;
run;

proc sql;
create table BHS_CARTAO_bs2 as (
select
NuCpfCnpj,
NuConta,
Base,
GH,
CPTO,
RU_Cartoes as RU_MODELO,
PD_BS as PD,
QtDiaAtrasoFaturaM,
LGD,
EAD,
. as PDLifeTime,
. as EADLifeTime,
. as LGDLifeTime,
'DXC' as  NmSistemaOrigem,
DTPROCESSBS as DtCorte
from BHS_CARTAO_bs1);
quit;

proc sql;
create table basesembs as (
select
a.NuCpfCnpj,
a.contanova1 as Nuconta,
. as Base,
. as GH,
'' as CPTO,
'Vetado' as RU_MODELO,
1  as PD,
.  as  QtDiaAtrasoFaturaM,
0.71   as LGD,/* Contingencia caso os parametros ainda vem como missing */
1.34  as EAD,/* Contingencia caso os parametros ainda vem como missing */
.  as PDLifeTime,
.  as EADLifeTime,
.  as LGDLifeTime,
'DXC' as  NmSistemaOrigem,
/*datepart(*/A.DtCorteFaturaM/*) format=ddmmyy10.*/
from Fatobehavior1 as a
left join BHS_CARTAO_BS2016_3D as b
on a.NuConta = b.Conta   and a.CdProduto=b.CdProduto
where b.conta =.);
quit;

data basesembs_1;
	set basesembs;
	DtCorte=datepart(DtCorteFaturaM);
	format DtCorte DDMMYY10.;
	/*DROP DtCorteFaturaM;*/
run;
data basesembs_1;
	set basesembs_1;
	DROP DtCorteFaturaM;
run;

data BHS_CARTAO_bs3;
set  BHS_CARTAO_bs2 basesembs_1;format DtProcessamentoOrigem ddmmyy10.;
DtProcessamentoOrigem ="&DtBasep."d;
run;

FILENAME BHSC "/projetos/ctrcredito/modelos/results/Behavior_Cartoes_IFRS_&Safra..txt" TERMSTR=NL;
PROC EXPORT DATA = BHS_CARTAO_bs3 (ENCODING=UTF8)
    OUTFILE=BHSC
    DBMS = dlm REPLACE;
    DELIMITER = ";";
RUN;
